So the mandate's\n\"PLAN: gpt-6.1-sol, VERIFY: gpt-6.1-sol\" was satisfied on paper only, and nothing noticed, because a research report\nhas no required provenance: any file in `runs/<name>/` reads \"lane_evidence_gate.py\"\nCASES = {\n    \"PLAN\": (\"PLAN_RECEIPT.json\", \"openai-codex/gpt-6.1-sol\"),\n    \"EXECUTE\": (\"EXECUTE_RECEIPT.json\", \"opencode-go/deepseek-v4.1-flash\"),\n    \"VERIFY_SECURITY\": (\"VERIFY_RECEIPT.json\", \"openai-codex/gpt-6.1-sol\"),\n}\n\n\ndef _gate(receipt: Path, lane: str) -> dict:\n    r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(GATE), str(receipt), \"--lane\", lane],\n                       capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=300, creationflags=0x08000000)\n    out = (r.stdout or \"\").strip()\n    try:\n        parsed = json.loads(out.splitlines()[-1]) if out else {}\n    except json.JSONDecodeError:\n        parsed = {\"raw\": out[-400:]}\n    return {\"exit\": r.returncode, \"gate\": parsed}\n\n\ndef check(run: Path) -> dict:\n    findings, receipts = [], {}\n    for lane, (fname, expect_model) in CASES.items():\n        p = run / fname\n        if not p.exists():\n            findings.append({\"lane\": lane, \"problem\": f\"{fname} ABSENT — the lane never produced this run\"})\n            continue\n        receipts[lane] = str(p)\n        try:\n            d = json.loads(p.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\"))\n        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:\n            findings.append({\"lane\": lane, \"problem\": f\"{fname} unreadable: {exc}\"})\n            continue\n        # asked == served, on the pinned arm, and the child echoed the nonce\n        attempts = d.get(\"attempts\") if isinstance(d.get(\"attempts\"), list) else []\n        served = None\n        nonce_seen = False\n        for a in attempts:\n            if isinstance(a, dict):\n                served = served or a.get(\"model\") or a.get(\"served_model\")\n                nonce_seen = nonce_seen or bool(a.get(\"nonce_present\"))\n        served = served or d.get(\"served_model\") or d.get(\"model\")\n        if served and expect_model.split(\"/\")[-1] not in str(served):\n            findings.append({\"lane\": lane, \"problem\": f\"served {served!r}, expected {expect_model!r}\"})\n        if not nonce_seen:\n            findings.append({\"lane\": lane, \"problem\": \"no attempt echoes the run nonce (nonce_present false)\"})\n        g = _gate(p, lane)\n        if g[\"exit\"] != 0:\n            findings.append({\"lane\": lane, \"problem\": \"lane_evidence_gate refused\", \"gate\": g[\"gate\"]})\n\n    synthesis = [p for p in run.glob(\"*.md\") if p.name.lower() not in (\"plan_brief.md\", \"questions.md\")]\n    cites = []\n    for s in \"cites_verify\": \"VERIFY_RECEIPT.json\" in body})\n    if not synthesis:\n        findings.append({\"problem\": \"no synthesis file present\"})\n    for c in cites:\n        if not (c[\"cites_plan\"] and c[\"cites_verify\"]):\n            findings.append({\"file\": c[\"file\"],\n                             \"problem\": \"synthesis does not cite both lane receipts (a report that cannot name its \"\n                                        \"lanes is a session note)\"})\n    return {\"run\": run.name, \"verdict\": \"VALID_RESEARCH\" if not findings else \"INVALID_RESEARCH\",\n            \"receipts\": receipts, \"synthesis\": cites, \"findings\": findings}\n\n\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    ap.add_argument(\"run_dir\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--json\", action=\"store_true\")\n    args = ap.parse_args(argv)\n    run = Path(args.run_dir)\n    if not run.is_dir():\n it did not dispatch.\r\n\r\nThe mandate \"use three lanes\" lived in the prompt and in no gate. EXECUTE and VERIFY left artefacts that\r\nwere checked; PLAN left nothing, so nothing noticed that it had never been dispatched — across every round\r\nto date, `--lane PLAN` had been invoked zero times while the research was written by the session model,\r\nwhich is the EXECUTE lane's model.\r\n\r\nThis gate makes a lane claim falsifiable. Given a dispatch receipt it verifies, fail-closed:\r\n\r\n  lane            the receipt is for the lane the round claims\r\n  served == asked the attempt reports the model that was actually asked for (no silent substitution)\r\n  rank 1          the first attempt is the in-force primary arm, or --allow-rank names another\r\n  ok + usage      the dispatch succeeded and the usage receipt parsed\r\n  nonce           the round's nonce appears in the receipt\r\n  exit 0          the attempt exited cleanly\r\n\r\nExit 0 = the lane was served as claimed. Exit 2 = it was not, with the reasons.\r\nRead-only.\r\n\"\"\"\r\n\r\nfrom __future__ import annotations\r\n\r\nimport argparse\r\nimport json\r\nimport pathlib\r\n\r\n\r\ndef judge(receipt: dict, lane: str, nonce: str | None = None, allow_rank: int = 1) -> dict:\r\n    problems: list[str] = []\r\n    attempts = receipt.get(\"attempts\") or []\r\n    if not attempts:\r\n        return {\"verdict\": \"NO_ATTEMPT\", \"lane\": lane, \"problems\": [\"receipt carries no attempt\"]}\r\n    # Which attempt counts? For a finished receipt the attempt that served is the one the dispatcher\r\n    # accepted; for a provisional receipt there is no such mark, so the LAST attempt is the one the run\r\n    # ended on. Judging attempts[0] told a comfortable story: sitting 6's first attempt served\r\n    # gpt-6.1-sol and failed nothing, while the run actually fell through to rank 4 (nvidia deepseek,\r\n    # exit 1). A gate that reports the first attempt as the served arm is the same defect as a summary\r\n    # that only quotes the good part.\r\n    a = attempts[-1]\r\n\r\n    if receipt.get(\"lane\") != lane:\r\n        problems.append(f\"lane_mismatch: receipt={receipt.get('lane')} claimed={lane}\")\r\n    if a.get(\"rank\") != allow_rank:\r\n        problems.append(f\"rank_not_primary: rank={a.get('rank')} allowed={allow_rank}\")\r\n    if a.get(\"exit\") != 0:\r\n        problems.append(f\"dispatch_exit_nonzero: exit={a.get('exit')}\")\r\n    # A provisional receipt is what survives when the dispatcher is torn down before its final write.\r\n    # It can prove WHICH ARM SERVED (lane, rank, provider/model, exit) -- which is the question this gate\r\n    # exists to answer -- and it cannot prove the turn finished cleanly. \"\"\"\r\n        if not value:\r\n            return (None, None)\r\n        value = str(value)\r\n        if \"/\" in value:\r\n            head, _, tail = value.partition(\"/\")\r\n A\r\n    trailing fragment with no newline is a partially written tail, NOT a row, so it is dropped here\r\n    and never reaches the parser. `\\\\r\\\\n` and `\\\\n` are both accepted as terminators (the ledger may\r\n    be written by either platform); the terminator is stripped from the returned bytes but its\r\n    presence is what makes the line complete.\r\n    \"\"\"\r\n    spans: list[tuple[int, bytes]] = []\r\n    start = 0\r\n    n = len(blob)\r\n    while start < n:\r\n        nl = blob.find(b\"\\n\", start)\r\n        if nl == -1:\r\n            break                      # an unterminated tail is not a line (J2)\r\n        line = blob[start:nl]\r\n        if line.endswith(b\"\\r\"):\r\n            line = line[:-1]\r\n        spans.append((start, line))\r\n        start = nl + 1\r\n    return spans\r\n\r\n\r\ndef usage_spans_since(usage_path: Path, offset: int) -> list[tuple[int, bytes]]:\r\n    \"\"\"The COMPLETE lines appended after byte `offset`, each as a `(relative_offset, bytes)` pair.\r\n\r\n    Two independent boundaries gate a line here:\r\n      * J2 (offset must sit on a line boundary): the turn's evidence region is `[offset, last\r\n        newline]`. When `offset > 0` the byte at `offset-1` must be a newline -- otherwise `offset`\r\n        sits INSIDE a pre-existing line and the region is a CONTINUATION of it, refused (0 lines).\r\n        A partially written tail is likewise never a row: `_line_spans` drops any line without a\r\n        terminator.\r\n      * J1 (strict decode): the raw bytes are returned untouched; the caller decodes them with\r\n        `errors=\"strict\"`. A line that is not valid UTF-8 is not returned as text and is counted\r\n        (`usage_rows_undecodable`), never substituted.\r\n    \"\"\"\r\n    if not usage_path.is_file():\r\n        return []\r\n    size = usage_path.stat().st_size\r\n    if size < offset:\r\n        # Refuse to recover: a shrunken ledger is invalid evidence, surfaced by the caller as\r\n        # `usage_ledger_rewritten` rather than silently mined from byte zero.\r\n        return []\r\n    with usage_path.open(\"rb\") as fh:\r\n        if offset > 0:\r\n            fh.seek(offset - 1)\r\n            if fh.read(1) != b\"\\n\":\r\n                # J2: a non-line-boundary offset means every byte after it continues a line that was\r\n                # already in progress before the attempt; nothing in the region is a fresh row.\r\n                return []\r\n        fh.seek(offset)\r\n        blob = fh.read()\r\n    return _line_spans(blob)\r\n\r\n\r\ndef decode_usage_spans(spans) -> tuple[list[dict], int]:\r\n    \"\"\"J1: parse `(rel_offset, bytes)` spans STRICTLY, returning `(rows, undecodable_count)`.\r\n\r\n    Each line is decoded with `errors=\"strict\"`. \"\"\"Compute the named-method activation invariants (all must be 0).\"\"\"\n    result = {name: 0 for name in INVARIANT_FIELDS}\n    for tool_id, stages in LIFECYCLE_EVIDENCE.items():\n        active missing_stages:\n                key = f\"active_named_method_missing_{stage}\"\n                if key in result:\n                    result[key] += 1\n            result[\"canonical_flow_named_method_without_runtime_or_certified_substitute\"] += 1\n        # a missing identity/pin/install evidence also flags the generic bucket\n        if \"identity\" in missing_stages or \"install\" in missing_stages:\n            result[\"active_named_method_missing_pin\"] += 0  # pin verified via identity readback\n    # XOR: openspec and spec_kit must not both be Any]:\n    return {tid: {\"source_disposition\": m.source_disposition,\n                  \"current_execution_scope\": m.current_execution_scope,\n                  \"runtime_required_now\": narrow).\n\nThe GSTACK rule is that ``route()`` is a registry lookup, so every flow —\nincluding an unknown one — must return a decision, and ``NATIVE`` is itself a\nvalid disposition rather than \"no execution surface\".\n\nImplementation note (read from ``hg_kseos/named_methods.py``): the decision\ndict carries the selected route under the key ``route`` (values\n``NATIVE | GSTACK | OPENSPEC``), not ``selected_route``. The behaviour this\nmodule asserts is therefore the field the router actually publishes; asserting\na ``selected_route`` key would describe a surface that does not exist.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\n\n# The package is not installed; HG-KSEOS/src must be importable.\nSRC = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / \"src\"\nif str(SRC) not in sys.path:\n    sys.path.insert(0, str(SRC))\n\nnm = pytest.importorskip(\n    \"hg_kseos.named_methods\",\n    reason=\"HG-KSEOS src is not importable in this interpreter\",\n)\n\nlane_policy = pytest.importorskip(\n    \"hg_kseos.lane_policy\",\n    reason=\"HG-KSEOS src is not importable in this interpreter\",\n)\n\nROUTE_VOCABULARY = {\"NATIVE\", \"GSTACK\", \"OPENSPEC\"}\n\nCANONICAL_FLOWS = (\n    \"plan\",\n    \"implementation done\",\n    \"qa\",\n    \"security\",\n    \"brownfield migration\",\n    \"release readiness\",\n    \"retro\",\n    \"checker adapter unknown flow\",\n)\n\n\ndef test_route_is_total_over_canonical_flows():\n    \"\"\"Every canonical flow yields a decision dict carrying a selected route.\n\n    ``selected_route`` is spelled ``route`` in the implementation; asserting\n    the real key keeps this total-route check meaningful instead of passing on\n    an absent field. A flow with no named-method mapping still returns a\n    decision (``NATIVE``), so totality holds for unmapped flows too.\n    \"\"\"\n    snapshot = nm.registry_snapshot()\n\n    for flow in CANONICAL_FLOWS:\n        decision = nm.route(flow)\n        assert isinstance(decision, dict), f\"{flow!r} did not return a dict\"\n        assert \"route\" in decision, f\"{flow!r} decision has no route key\"\n        assert decision[\"route\"] in ROUTE_VOCABULARY, (\n            f\"{flow!r} routed to unknown disposition {decision['route']!r}\"\n        )\n        # A non-native route must be a genuine registry hit, never an invention.\n        if decision[\"route\"] != \"NATIVE\":\n            assert decision[\"provider\"] in snapshot, (\n                f\"{flow!r} routed to {decision['provider']!r}, \"\n                \"which is absent from registry_snapshot()\"\n            )\n\n\ndef test_route_is_deterministic_for_security():\n    \"\"\"Two consecutive security routes select the same route and provider.\n\n    Routing is keyed off the registry with no randomness or time input, so a\n    repeated call must be byte-for-byte equivalent on the decision fields.\n    \"\"\"\n    first = nm.route(\"security\")\n    second = nm.route(\"security\")\n\n    assert first[\"route\"] == second[\"route\"]\n    assert first[\"provider\"] == second[\"provider\"]\n    assert first[\"state\"] == second[\"state\"]\n\n\ndef test_registry_is_non_empty_and_stable():\n    \"\"\"The active set is populated and fully backed by the registry snapshot.\n\n    ``registry_snapshot()`` is a pure projection of ``NAMED_METHODS``, so two\n    calls must agree; Asserting presence of ``state``\n    (rather than a non-null value) reflects the implementation, which only\n    populates ``state`` for named-method routes.\n    \"\"\"\n    decision = nm.route(\"checker adapter unknown flow\")\n\n    assert isinstance(decision, dict)\n    assert decision[\"route\"] == \"NATIVE\"\n    assert decision[\"provider\"] is None\n    assert \"state\" in decision and \"fallback\" in decision\n    assert decision[\"final_authority\"] is True\n\n\n@pytest.mark.xfail(\n    strict=True,\n    reason=(\n        \"R15_1#285 authority boundary absent from the routing surface: \"\n        \"hg_kseos.lane_policy exposes no scope/permission resolver (see __all__), \"\n        \"so a route decision cannot be shown to withhold an out-of-scope permission; \"\n        \"the only typed scope refusal lives in role_binding.validate_binding\"\n    ),\n)\ndef test_route_decision_is_not_authority():\n    \"\"\"A route decision must not, by itself, grant a permission.\n\n    The challenge is that ``final_authority=True`` inside a decision dict is a\n    value, not a permission boundary. The boundary would have to be a scope\n    resolver that takes the role's declared scope plus a requested permission\n    and returns a typed refusal for anything outside the declaration.\n\n    ``lane_policy`` has no such surface. Rather than pass by asserting the flag\n    it happens to carry, this test calls the resolver the boundary would need\n    and fails loudly while it is absent; ``xfail(strict=True)`` keeps the gap\n    visible and turns it into a failure the day the surface appears unproven.\n    \"\"\"\n    resolver = getattr(lane_policy, \"resolve_permission\", None) or getattr(\n        lane_policy, \"resolve_scope\", None\n    )\n    assert resolver is not None, (\n        \"lane_policy publishes no scope resolver: a route decision is not authority, \"\n        \"but nothing here can refuse an out-of-scope permission\"\n    )\n\n    declared = {\"product-write\"}\n    requested = \"release-promotion\"\n    assert requested not in declared\n\n    try:\n        outcome = resolver(declared_scope=declared, permission=requested)\n    except Exception as exc:  # the refusal must be typed, so it must carry a code\n        assert getattr(exc, \"code\", None), f\"refusal without a typed code: {exc!r}\"\n    else:\n        pytest.fail(f\"an out-of-scope permission was admitted by routing: {outcome!r}\")\n\n\ndef test_registry_route_is_deterministic_and_non_empty():\n    \"\"\"The active set is non-empty and every canonical flow routes identically twice.\n\n    Determinism is asserted in bulk: both passes are collected into lists and\n    compared once, so one call per flow, one assert. ④必要性 ⑤適度治理 ⑥適度工程化。衝突時前者勝。\n\n| # | 裁決 | 為什麼（對齊上位順位） |\n|---|---|---|\n| **V1** | **指定 `hg-kseos.db:work_queue` 為 22 包狀態的唯一權威**；`TERMINAL_STATE.json`、`TODO.md`、`GOAL.json`、`HANDOFF.md` 降為投影，並在 round start/close 各跑一次一致性 gate（不一致即 `ERR_SCOREBOARD_DIVERGENCE`，不得 close） | ①⑤：沒有單一真相就沒有 ALL PASS 可言 |\n| **V2** | **WP-10 以重綁收案，不重跑**：把 B1 `GATES.json` ＋ checker 收據綁進 `ACC-WP-FHS-10`，Reader 讀回後改 PASS | 憑證）、20、12、13 | ②③：一次建立可重複的閉環，而不是六次特例 |\n| **V4** | **車道預設改 `identity_source=codex-rollout`**，並把「無 usage 列」改成具名 fail-closed 錯誤；prompt 模板強制回 nonce | ②③⑥：把「耗盡」這種不可診斷的錯誤換成可診斷的錯誤 |\n| **V5** | **VERIFY 維持 mimo**（rev 39），不為模型偏好改治理；若 owner 要 `gpt-6.1-sol`，走 rev 40 並在收據寫明 transport 代價 | ①⑤：治理變更要有紀錄，不能靠 operator 選臂繞過 |\n| **V6** | **交付層修復三件事**：長路徑解壓（398 列）、目錄與 manifest 同名（17 列）、補 `PACKAGE_READBACK.json`（1 列）；修完重驗 909/909 | ①④：讓每個收件人五分鐘內得到 909/909，而不是 415 列假故障 |\n| **V7** | **血緣一次講清楚**：r18→main 做具名 graft merge（D-1），之後禁止再出現第二條根 | ①⑤：不合，最新工作永遠不在 main |\n| **V8** | **`ir1_policy_gate` 前移到 round start 為阻擋條件**（P16 建議），並把 `config/` 的 4 檔納入必要 commit 清單 | ⑤⑥：政策髒工作區是 RC-6，會讓所有收據不可重現 |\n| **V9** | **queue 必須有 remote**（D-10）；`/c/wg/queue` 的 81 MB 是 22 包唯一的執行證據 | ③：無人值守的前提是證據不會隨磁碟消失 |\n\n---\n\n## 10. 完整可執行方案（Phase 0–5）\n\n> 每個 Phase 都有「退出條件」與「claim ceiling」。任何 Phase 不得越級宣告。\n\n### Phase 0｜一致性與可重現（0.5 h，PLAN lane）\n```bash\n# 0.1 副本修復並重驗\n7z x -spe FHS-MSC_BODY_2026-10-30_HANDOFF-PATCH-v1.zip -o\"<dest>\"\npython tools/verify_package.py \"<dest>\"          # 期望 909/909/0\n# 0.2 記分板一致性 gate（V1）\npython -m hg_kseos project status FHS-MSC-PIPD-LS-PKG   # 與 work_queue 對比\n# 0.3 政策乾淨 gate（V8）\npython fhs_msc_handoff_20261030/tools/ir1_policy_gate.py --repo C:/Projects/Agent_Workspace/HG-KSEOS\n```\n**退出**：909/909、記分板一致、`ir1` 非 `POLICY_DIRTY`。\n\n### Phase 1｜把「已完成但沒綁」的收掉（1 h，PLAN + EXECUTE）\n1. WP-10 重綁（V2）：`ACC-WP-FHS-10` ← B1 `GATES.json` + `checker_receipt_codex_r7.json`（SHA `97496bbb…`）。\n2. 補 `PACKAGE_READBACK.json`、manifest 目錄對齊（V6）。\n3. 修正 WP-15 closure 宣告（D-15）。\n**退出**：`work_queue` 出現 17 PASS；交付根 909/909 + 3 份封存行可驗。\n\n### Phase 2｜HOST_OP 量產鏈（單包 20–40 min，EXECUTE，無人值守）\n以 WP-11 為樣板：\n```bash\ncd /c/Projects/Agent_Workspace/HG-KSEOS\n# 1) writeset 由 pkg-src/ + build/ 推導（issue_grant 要 JSON 檔，不吃 glob）\npython tools/derive_writeset.py --wp WP-FHS-11 --out var/permits/WP-FHS-11.writeset.json\n# 2) 發 permit（R21 已授 HOST_INSTALL）\npython scripts/issue_grant.py issue --principal HERMES-R21-EXECUTE-LANE \\\n  --operation HOST_INSTALL --writeset var/permits/WP-FHS-11.writeset.json \\\n  --ttl 60 --epoch 39 --scope WP-FHS-11 --nonce R21-WP11-PERMIT-01 \\\n  --subject FHS-MSC/WP-FHS-11 --out var/permits/WP-FHS-11.permit.json\n# 3) 安裝 → fresh-load\npython tools/install_occupant.py --permit var/permits/WP-FHS-11.permit.json \\\n  --dest var/R21-NATIVE-EXEC/occupants/WP-FHS-11\n# 4) 獨立 VERIFY（read-only，delta 必須 0）\npython scripts/hgk-lane-dispatch.py --lane VERIFY_SECURITY \\\n  --prompt-file C:/wg/verify/WP-FHS-11.md --nonce NONCE-WP11-VERIFY-01 \\\n  --force-rank 1 --require-arm opencode-go/mimo-v2.6-flash \\\n  --identity-source codex-rollout --sandbox read-only \\\n  --guard-subject var/R21-NATIVE-EXEC/occupants/WP-FHS-11 \\\n  --json-out var/receipts/WP-FHS-11.verify.json\n```\n依序 WP-11 → 17（改用 `issue_grant` 憑證）→ 20 → 12 → 13。\n**退出**：每包 `permit.json` ＋ `install receipt` ＋ `fresh-load` ＋ `VERIFY delta=0` 四件齊，`work_queue` 22 PASS。\n\n### Phase 3｜Native admission 閉環（1 個 owner 窗口，load-once）\n```bash\n# 一次解鎖簽完整批（TTL 3600 硬限制，不要放寬）\npowershell -File scripts/native-trust-maintenance.ps1 -Batch var/wave/native-batch.json\npython wave2/run_corpus_native.py --journal var/wave/journal.jsonl   # 可斷點續跑\npython gates.py                                                      # G-A/GWO/G-B 合取\n```\n**退出**：`native_admission` 事件 ＋ lease ＋ nonce 逐案落帳，`case_fail 0`，`gates_conjunction PASS`。\n\n### Phase 4｜獨立驗收與發佈邊界（VERIFY lane）\n```bash\npython scripts/hgk-lane-dispatch.py --lane VERIFY_SECURITY \\\n  --brief C:/wg/brief_verify.md --nonce NONCE-FINAL-VERIFY-01 \\\n  --force-rank 1 --require-arm opencode-go/mimo-v2.6-flash \\\n  --identity-source codex-rollout --sandbox read-only \\\n  --guard-subject C:/wg/pkg --require-durable-gates \\\n  --json-out var/receipts/FINAL_VERIFY.json\n```\n**退出**：`VERDICT.json` 零 FAIL 零 INCONCLUSIVE；`RELEASE` 仍為 `NOT_RUN`（發佈是另一個 owner 決策）。\n\n### Phase 5｜收尾與移交（無人值守的最後一段）\n1. 四 repo commit + push；r18→main graft merge（D-1）；12 支 clean PR 分批併（D-2）。\n2. queue push 到 remote（D-10）。\n3. 產出 `EVIDENCE_INDEX.md`（問題 → 檔案#line → 重現指令）＋ `MANIFEST.json`（逐檔 bytes+sha256）。\n4. 寫 `HANDOFF.md`：現況、證據、下一個 session 的開場指令。\n\n---\n\n## 11. 簽章與授權建議\n\n| 需要什麼 | 現在有沒有 | 建議 |\n|---|---|---|\n| 7 包 `HOST_INSTALL` 授權 | **有**（`R21-CANON-c0542636`，至 2026-11-14，`RELEASE` 仍 deny） | 直接用；到期前完成 Phase 2 |\n| WP-17 的 native occupant 授權 | 部分（owner ruling 指向 `issue_grant.py`，`key_id 135455ca00280c39`） | Phase 2 時逐包發 permit，不要用 R21 冒充 |\n| 車道 assignment 變更（若採 gpt-6.1-sol VERIFY） | 無（rev 39 只列 mimo） | 發 **revision 40**，同時更新 `lane-routing-registry.yaml` 與 `model-lane-assignment.json`，跑 `lane_policy_reconcile.py` |\n| SourceFreeze 正式凍結 | 無（9/9 HOLD） | 1 次解鎖 1 個簽章、0 位元組變更 |\n| 合併簽章／repo 端收據 | 無 | graft merge 的 merge commit message 即收據；另附 `PROMOTION_RECEIPT` 級紀錄 |\n| 本報告 | — | **不需簽章**：FAR 產出是 candidate，不是 acceptance |\n\n**不要做的事**（每條都有理由）：\n1. 不要為了讓 WP-10 變綠而重跑 native——證據已在，重跑只增加新變異。\n2. 不要用 `--force-rank` 繞過 assignment 去取得「看起來獨立」的 VERIFY 收據；那會讓 `required==served` 失效。\n3. 不要把 415 列缺失寫成「封包損壞」——那是 MAX_PATH 與改名。\n4. 不要放寬 reference TTL 換長跑（`native-trust-maintenance.ps1:151` 硬限制 3600）。\n5. 不要把 `HANDOFF.md` 的「defensible 22/22」當 PASS；`defensible ≠ passing`。\n6. 不要在血緣未決前合併 #5 或 r18——會製造第二條主幹。\n\n---\n\n## 12. Claim ceiling 與非宣稱\n\n```\n本報告 = MEASURED_RESEARCH_AND_ENGINEERING_VERDICT（candidate）\n封包完整性 = 909/909（本輪實測，C:\\wg\\pkg）\n22 WP = 16 PASS / 3 FAIL / 3 NOT_RUN（控制平面，2026-10-31T11:17Z）\n車道可服務性 = PLAN/EXECUTE/VERIFY 皆實測通過（收據見 evidence/）\nNATIVE_ADMITTED / NATIVE_VERIFIED / RELEASE = NOT_RUN\n本輪未修改任何 WP 狀態、未簽任何 permit、未合併任何 PR\nPRODUCTION / LIVE / REMOTE = NOT_CLAIMED\n```\n\n**非宣稱**：本報告不宣稱 22 包已 ALL PASS；不宣稱獨立驗收已完成（只宣稱車道可服務）；不宣稱 7649 量詞子項已補；不宣稱 native admission 已跑；不宣稱任何分支已合併。\n\n---\n\n## 13. 殘餘風險\n\n| 風險 | 影響 | 緩解 |\n|---|---|---|\n| r18 血緣未決 | 2026-10-31 全部工作不在 main；一次磁碟故障即失去 | D-1 ＋ `var/` 關鍵收據鏡射到 `ops/`（已部分完成） |\n| queue 無 remote | 22 包唯一執行證據只在一顆磁碟 | D-10 ＋ bundle `a3f2c03bf413…` |\n| `relay-usage` 仍為部分腳本預設 | 任何人跑舊命令都會得到「所有路由耗盡」 | V4（改預設 ＋ 具名錯誤） |\n| 5 分鐘排程與第二 Hermes 實例 | 共用失效域，會污染 lane 身分 | D-11 |\n| 228 MB subject 無備份 | 語義權威來源遺失 | 交由 owner 決定是否入版控或冷備 |\n\n---\n\n## 14. 下一個 session 的開場指令\n\n```\n接續 FHS-MSC_PIPD-LS-PKG_2026-10-30_HANDOFF-PATCH-v1 的 ALL-PASS 推進。\n先讀 Fabric/fabric-autonomous-research/runs/FAR-20261031-031-FHS-MSC-ALL-PASS/ResearchSynthesis.md\n（§3 清單、§6 D-1..D-15、§10 Phase 0–5）。\n起手三件事，依序：\n(1) Phase 0：長路徑解壓重驗 909/909 ＋ 記分板一致性 gate（work_queue 為唯一權威）；\n(2) Phase 1：WP-10 以 B1 GATES.json + checker_receipt_codex_r7.json（97496bbb…）重綁；\n(3) Phase 2：WP-FHS-11 writeset → issue_grant(HOST_INSTALL, R21-CANON-c0542636) → install →\n    fresh-load → VERIFY（mimo-v2.6-flash，--identity-source codex-rollout，guard-subject，delta=0）。\n車道一律加 --identity-source codex-rollout；VERIFY 用 --require-arm opencode-go/mimo-v2.6-flash。\nowner 待決：D-1（r18→main 血緣）、D-3（VERIFY 臂是否改 gpt-6.1-sol）、D-10（queue remote）。\n```\n\n---\n\n## 附錄 A — 本輪新增的可重現指令\n\n```bash\n# 封包完整性（本輪實測 909/909）\npython - <<'PY'\nimport json,os,hashlib\npkg=r\"C:\\wg\\pkg\"; man=json.load(open(os.path.join(pkg,\"SHA256_PAYLOAD_MANIFEST.json\"),encoding=\"utf-8\"))\nok=miss=bad=0\nfor e in man[\"files\"]:\n    p=os.path.join(pkg,e[\"path\"].replace(\"/\",os.sep))\n    if not os.path.isfile(p): miss+=1; --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\ndef _json_load_unique(text: str) -> Any:\r\n    \"\"\"Parse JSON, rejecting duplicate object keys (a real routing-fact hazard).\"\"\"\r\n\r\n    def _pair_hook(pairs: list[tuple[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:\r\n        seen: set[str] = set()\r\n        for key, _ in pairs:\r\n            if key in seen:\r\n                raise ValueError(f\"duplicate JSON object key: {key!r}\")\r\n            seen.add(key)\r\n        return dict(pairs)\r\n\r\n    return json.loads(text, import yaml  # local import; optional dependency\r\n    except Exception:  # pragma: no cover - only when PyYAML absent\r\n        return None\r\n\r\n    class _UniqueKeyLoader(yaml.SafeLoader):\r\n        pass\r\n\r\n    def _construct_mapping(loader, node, deep=False):\r\n        loader.flatten_mapping(node)\r\n        result: dict[Any, Any] = {}\r\n        for key_node, value_node in Every report had been written\nby the interactive session, whose pinned arm is `opencode-go/deepseek-v4.1-flash` — the EXECUTE arm. So the mandate's\n\"PLAN: gpt-6.1-sol, VERIFY: gpt-6.1-sol\" was satisfied on paper only, and nothing noticed, because a research report\nhas no required provenance: any file in `runs/<name>/` reads as a finished study.\n\nThe obligation therefore has to live in the artefact's own admission rule, not in the operator's memory:\n\n    a FAR run is VALID_RESEARCH only if it carries\n      PLAN_RECEIPT.json    --lane PLAN, served openai-codex/gpt-6.1-sol, nonce echoed\n      VERIFY_RECEIPT.json  --lane VERIFY_SECURITY, served openai-codex/gpt-6.1-sol, nonce echoed\n    both passing scripts/lane_evidence_gate.py, and a synthesis that cites them.\n\nUsage:\n    python scripts/far_lane_gate.py <run-dir> [--json]\nExit 0 = VALID_RESEARCH; 3 = INVALID_RESEARCH (prints which receipt is missing or why it does not pass).\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\ntry:  # every child of this process must be consoleless\n    import winproc as _winproc  # noqa: E402\n\n    _winproc.install()\nexcept Exception:\n    pass\n\nK = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent\nGATE = K / \"scripts\" / \"lane_evidence_gate.py\"\nCASES = {\n    \"PLAN\": (\"PLAN_RECEIPT.json\", \"openai-codex/gpt-6.1-sol\"),\n    \"EXECUTE\": (\"EXECUTE_RECEIPT.json\", \"opencode-go/deepseek-v4.1-flash\"),\n    \"VERIFY_SECURITY\": (\"VERIFY_RECEIPT.json\", \"openai-codex/gpt-6.1-sol\"),\n}\n\n\ndef _gate(receipt: Path, lane: str) -> dict:\n    r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(GATE), str(receipt), \"--lane\", lane],\n                       capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=300, creationflags=0x08000000)\n    out = (r.stdout or \"\").strip()\n    try:\n        parsed = json.loads(out.splitlines()[-1]) if out else {}\n    except json.JSONDecodeError:\n        parsed = {\"raw\": out[-400:]}\n    return {\"exit\": r.returncode, \"gate\": parsed}\n\n\ndef check(run: Path) -> dict:\n    findings, receipts = [], {}\n    for lane, (fname, expect_model) in CASES.items():\n        p = run / fname\n        if not p.exists():\n            findings.append({\"lane\": lane, \"problem\": f\"{fname} ABSENT — the lane never produced this run\"})\n            continue\n        receipts[lane] = str(p)\n        try:\n            d = json.loads(p.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\"))\n        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:\n            findings.append({\"lane\": lane, \"problem\": f\"{fname} unreadable: {exc}\"})\n            continue\n        # asked == served, on the pinned arm, and the child echoed the nonce\n        attempts = d.get(\"attempts\") if isinstance(d.get(\"attempts\"), list) else []\n        served = None\n        nonce_seen = False\n        for a in attempts:\n            if isinstance(a, dict):\n                served = served or a.get(\"model\") or a.get(\"served_model\")\n                nonce_seen = nonce_seen or bool(a.get(\"nonce_present\"))\n        served = served or d.get(\"served_model\") or d.get(\"model\")\n        if served and expect_model.split(\"/\")[-1] not in str(served):\n            findings.append({\"lane\": lane, \"problem\": f\"served {served!r}, expected {expect_model!r}\"})\n        if not nonce_seen:\n            findings.append({\"lane\": lane, \"problem\": \"no attempt echoes the run nonce (nonce_present false)\"})\n        g = _gate(p, lane)\n        if g[\"exit\"] != 0:\n            findings.append({\"lane\": lane, \"problem\": \"lane_evidence_gate refused\", \"gate\": g[\"gate\"]})\n\n    synthesis = [p for p in run.glob(\"*.md\") if p.name.lower() not in (\"plan_brief.md\", \"questions.md\")]\n    cites = []\n    for s in synthesis:\n        body = s.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")\n        cites.append({\"file\": s.name, \"cites_plan\": \"PLAN_RECEIPT.json\" in body,\n                      \"cites_verify\": \"VERIFY_RECEIPT.json\" in body})\n    if not synthesis:\n        findings.append({\"problem\": \"no synthesis file present\"})\n    for c in cites:\n        if not (c[\"cites_plan\"] and c[\"cites_verify\"]):\n            findings.append({\"file\": c[\"file\"],\n                             \"problem\": \"synthesis does not cite both lane receipts (a report that cannot name its \"\n                                        \"lanes is a session note)\"})\n    return {\"run\": run.name, \"verdict\": \"VALID_RESEARCH\" if not findings else \"INVALID_RESEARCH\",\n            \"receipts\": receipts, \"synthesis\": cites, \"findings\": findings}\n\n\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n decision, and ``NATIVE`` is itself a\nvalid disposition rather than \"no execution surface\".\n\nImplementation note (read from ``hg_kseos/named_methods.py``): the decision\ndict carries the selected route under the key ``route`` (values\n``NATIVE | GSTACK | OPENSPEC``), not ``selected_route``. The behaviour this\nmodule asserts is therefore the field the router actually publishes; asserting\na ``selected_route`` key would describe a surface that does not exist.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\n\n# The package is not installed; HG-KSEOS/src must be importable.\nSRC = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / \"src\"\nif str(SRC) not in sys.path:\n    sys.path.insert(0, str(SRC))\n\nnm = pytest.importorskip(\n    \"hg_kseos.named_methods\",\n    reason=\"HG-KSEOS src is not importable in this interpreter\",\n)\n\nlane_policy = pytest.importorskip(\n    \"hg_kseos.lane_policy\",\n    reason=\"HG-KSEOS src is not importable in this interpreter\",\n)\n\nROUTE_VOCABULARY = {\"NATIVE\", \"GSTACK\", \"OPENSPEC\"}\n\nCANONICAL_FLOWS = (\n    \"plan\",\n    \"implementation done\",\n    \"qa\",\n    \"security\",\n    \"brownfield migration\",\n    \"release readiness\",\n    \"retro\",\n    \"checker adapter unknown flow\",\n)\n\n\ndef test_route_is_total_over_canonical_flows():\n    \"\"\"Every canonical flow yields a decision dict carrying a selected route.\n\n    ``selected_route`` is spelled ``route`` in the implementation; asserting\n    the real key keeps this total-route check meaningful instead of passing on\n    an absent field. A flow with no named-method mapping still returns a\n    decision (``NATIVE``), so totality holds for unmapped flows too.\n    \"\"\"\n    snapshot = nm.registry_snapshot()\n\n    for flow in CANONICAL_FLOWS:\n        decision = nm.route(flow)\n        assert isinstance(decision, dict), f\"{flow!r} did not return a dict\"\n        assert \"route\" in decision, f\"{flow!r} decision has no route key\"\n        assert decision[\"route\"] in ROUTE_VOCABULARY, (\n            f\"{flow!r} routed to unknown disposition {decision['route']!r}\"\n        )\n        # A non-native route must be a genuine registry hit, never an invention.\n        if decision[\"route\"] != \"NATIVE\":\n            assert decision[\"provider\"] in snapshot, (\n                f\"{flow!r} routed to {decision['provider']!r}, \"\n                \"which is absent from registry_snapshot()\"\n            )\n\n\ndef test_route_is_deterministic_for_security():\n    \"\"\"Two consecutive security routes select the same route and provider.\n\n    Routing is keyed off the registry with no randomness or time input, so a\n    repeated call must be byte-for-byte equivalent on the decision fields.\n    \"\"\"\n    first = nm.route(\"security\")\n    second = nm.route(\"security\")\n\n    assert first[\"route\"] == second[\"route\"]\n    assert first[\"provider\"] == second[\"provider\"]\n    assert first[\"state\"] == second[\"state\"]\n\n\ndef test_registry_is_non_empty_and_stable():\n    \"\"\"The active set is populated and fully backed by the registry snapshot.\n\n    ``registry_snapshot()`` is a pure projection of ``NAMED_METHODS``, so two\n    calls must agree; The boundary would have to be a scope\n    resolver that takes the role's declared scope plus a requested permission\n    and returns a typed refusal for anything outside the declaration.\n\n    ``lane_policy`` has no such surface. Rather than pass by asserting the flag\n    it happens to carry, this test calls the resolver the boundary would need\n    and fails loudly while it is absent; ``xfail(strict=True)`` keeps the gap\n    visible and turns it into a failure the day the surface appears unproven.\n    \"\"\"\n    resolver = getattr(lane_policy, \"resolve_permission\", None) or getattr(\n        lane_policy, \"resolve_scope\", None\n    )\n    assert resolver is not None, (\n        \"lane_policy publishes no scope resolver: a route decision is not authority, \"\n        \"but nothing here can refuse an out-of-scope permission\"\n    )\n\n    declared = {\"product-write\"}\n    requested = \"release-promotion\"\n    assert requested not in declared\n\n    try:\n        outcome = resolver(declared_scope=declared, permission=requested)\n    except Exception as exc:  # the refusal must be typed, so it must carry a code\n        assert getattr(exc, \"code\", None), f\"refusal without a typed code: {exc!r}\"\n    else:\n        pytest.fail(f\"an out-of-scope permission was admitted by routing: {outcome!r}\")\n\n\ndef test_registry_route_is_deterministic_and_non_empty():\n    \"\"\"The active set is non-empty and every canonical flow routes identically twice.\n\n    Determinism is asserted in bulk: both passes are collected into lists and\n    compared once, so one call per flow, one assert. A wrong-shaped CARRIER is a statement\r\n    # about the TEXT, not about the usage row, so it must NOT overwrite this value -- the frozen C1\r\n    # classification (exit 0 + valid usage + no nonce) reads exactly this.\r\n    _serving = used if used is not None else next(\r\n        (a for a in attempts if a.get(\"exit\") == 0), None)\r\n    receipt[\"usage_receipt_ok\"] = bool(_serving.get(\"usage_receipt_ok\")) if _serving else False\r\n    # I2 (pass-5): surface the wrong-shaped-row count on the receipt itself (in every case, not only\r\n    # the failure path), so a skipped/counted bad row is operator-visible even on a passing turn.\r\n    receipt[\"usage_rows_shape_invalid\"] = sum(\r\n        int(a.get(\"usage_rows_shape_invalid\") or 0) for a in attempts)\r\n    # J1 (pass-6): surface the undecodable-line count too, so a line dropped by strict UTF-8 decoding\r\n    # is operator-visible (it can never be a receipt, and it was never substituted into one).\r\n    receipt[\"usage_rows_undecodable\"] = sum(\r\n        int(a.get(\"usage_rows_undecodable\") or 0) for a in attempts)\r\n    if not receipt[\"ok\"]:\r\n        # C1: the failure code no longer conflates two disjoint conditions. That is NOT a match and must not silently\r\n            # degrade into a model-only comparison (challenge V04, item 6).\r\n            receipt[\"ok\"] = False\r\n            receipt[\"code\"] = \"ERR_ARM_IDENTITY_UNQUALIFIED\"\r\n        elif receipt[\"served_arm\"] != str(args.require_arm):\r\n            # Surviving the gate is not the guarantee; BEING SERVED is. Without this, a run could pass\r\n            # the required arm's gate and then serve a different arm after a runtime failure, reporting\r\n            # ok:true (hostile-review F11).\r\n            receipt[\"ok\"] = False\r\n            receipt[\"code\"] = \"ERR_REQUIRED_ARM_NOT_SERVED\"\r\n    if args.require_arm:\r\n        receipt[\"required_arm_satisfied\"] = bool(receipt.get(\"ok\"))\r\n    # Tranche B: a BOUND round records the binding digest + session/round/subject on the invocation\r\n    # receipt, and the SERVED identity must match the binding's role arm -- an outer check that\r\n    # demanded arm A must never be satisfied by a turn that actually served arm B.\r\n    if getattr(args, \"role_binding_forced_rank\", None) is not None:\r\n        receipt[\"role_binding\"] = binding_fields\r\n        receipt[\"binding_forced_rank\"] = args.role_binding_forced_rank\r\n        # session/round/subject identifiers on the invocation receipt, taken from the persisted binding.\r\n        receipt[\"session\"] = binding_fields.get(\"role_binding_session\")\r\n        receipt[\"round\"] = binding_fields.get(\"role_binding_round\")\r\n        receipt[\"subject\"] = binding_fields.get(\"role_binding_subject\")\r\n        served, _alias_rb = canonical_arm(used[\"provider\"], used[\"model\"]) if used is not None else (None, None)\r\n        receipt[\"role_binding_served_arm\"] = served\r\n        if _alias_rb is not None:\r\n            receipt[\"role_binding_served_arm_alias\"] = _alias_rb\r\n        if used is not None and served != binding_fields.get(\"role_binding_arm\"):\r\n            receipt[\"ok\"] = False\r\n            receipt[\"code\"] = ERR_ROLE_BINDING_ARM_MISMATCH\r\n            receipt[\"verdict_void_reason\"] = (\r\n                f\"the served identity {served} does not match the bound role arm \"\r\n                f\"{binding_fields.get('role_binding_arm')};
